\begin{filecontents*}[overwrite]{efv_README.txt}
The sharp anticanonical volume bound for exceptional Fano surfaces
Unsigned manuscript. Completed 13 November 2026.
Public-review presentation prepared 21 November 2026.
Mostly generated by AI. Under verification.
The person sharing this manuscript claims no personal credit for it or its results.

Run from any directory, with Python 3.9 or later (standard library only):
  python3 efv_verify_nonvanishing.py
  python3 efv_verify_sharp.py
  python3 efv_audit.py
  python3 efv_check_printed_table.py
Do not use Python's -O option, which disables assertions.

The local catalogues are regenerated, not read from files.
The JSON input files contain rational linear multipliers.
All inequalities and constants are evaluated with exact arithmetic.
No optimization solver, previous report, or private mathematical reference is required.
The programs write four JSON result files. Execution times are machine dependent.

The manuscript proves the completeness of the local parameter ranges and
the validity of the geometric inequalities used in these calculations.
Passing the checks is arithmetic verification, not formal verification of those proofs.

The single TeX source contains these files as filecontents environments.
Compile twice with pdflatex; no separate bibliography file or BibTeX is needed.
The PDF embeds the input programs and certificates as file attachments.
If a PDF viewer does not expose attachments, use the separate verification ZIP.
The mathematical text and bibliography precede the source-data block at the
end of the TeX source. The data block does not add visible pages to the PDF.
\end{filecontents*}

\begin{filecontents*}[overwrite]{efv_audit.py}
"""Independent residue/graph verification of the sharp-volume dual.
Standard library only; local corrections are recomputed independently.
"""
import ast,json,sys,time
from pathlib import Path
from fractions import Fraction as F
from math import gcd,lcm
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
from efv_local_catalogue import A,W,S,U,cyclic_cost_numerator,catalogue

def inverse_and_det(matrix):
    n=len(matrix)
    a=[[F(v) for v in row]+[F(int(i==j)) for j in range(n)] for i,row in enumerate(matrix)]
    det=F(1)
    for i in range(n):
        k=next(k for k in range(i,n) if a[k][i])
        if k!=i:a[k],a[i]=a[i],a[k];det=-det
        pivot=a[i][i];det*=pivot;a[i]=[v/pivot for v in a[i]]
        for j in range(n):
            if j==i or not a[j][i]:continue
            mult=a[j][i];a[j]=[x-mult*y for x,y in zip(a[j],a[i])]
    return [row[n:] for row in a],det

def hj(r,q):
    out=[]
    while q:
        w=-(-r//q);out.append(w);r,q=q,w*q-r
    return out

def cyclic_independent(r,q):
    diag=hj(r,q);a0,a1=r,q+1;dis=[]
    for w in diag:
        dis.append(F(a1,r));a0,a1=a1,w*a1-a0
    assert a1==r
    if min(dis)<=F(1,11):return None
    gamma=len(diag)-sum((w-2)*(1-a) for w,a in zip(diag,dis))
    mu=gamma+1-F(1,r)
    qi=pow(q,-1,r);cum=[0]
    for j in range(1,r):cum.append(cum[-1]+r-1-2*(j*qi%r))
    period=r//gcd(r,q+1)
    cs=[F(cum[m*(q+1)%r],2*r) for m in range(period)]
    cost=F(A,S)*mu+sum(F(w,S)*cs[m%period] for m,w in enumerate(W,1))
    assert cost==F(cyclic_cost_numerator(r,q,len(diag),sum(w-2 for w in diag)),S*r)
    return gamma,mu,cs,cost

def fork_independent(name):
    e0,rest=name[2:-1].split(';');arms=ast.literal_eval(rest)
    diag=[int(e0)];edges=[]
    for r,q in arms:
        prev=0
        for w in hj(r,q):
            j=len(diag);diag.append(w);edges.append((prev,j));prev=j
    mat=[[0]*len(diag) for _ in diag]
    for i,w in enumerate(diag):mat[i][i]=w
    for i,j in edges:mat[i][j]=mat[j][i]=-1
    inv,_=inverse_and_det(mat);kv=[w-2 for w in diag]
    b=[sum(x*y for x,y in zip(row,kv)) for row in inv]
    assert min(1-x for x in b)>F(1,11)
    gamma=len(diag)-sum(x*y for x,y in zip(kv,b))
    eta=F(e0)-sum(F(q,r) for r,q in arms)
    sigma=sum(F(1,r) for r,q in arms)-1
    group=4*eta/sigma**2
    mu=gamma+1-1/group
    per=lcm(*(x.denominator for x in b));cs=[]
    for m in range(per):
        f=[(m+1)*x-((m+1)*x).__floor__() for x in b]
        quad=sum(f[i]*mat[i][j]*f[j] for i in range(len(diag)) for j in range(len(diag)))
        cs.append((sum(x*y for x,y in zip(kv,f))-quad)/2)
    cost=F(A,S)*mu+sum(F(w,S)*cs[m%per] for m,w in enumerate(W,1))
    return gamma,mu,cs,cost

def main():
    start=time.time();G=catalogue();prep=[]
    for g in G:
        if g['name'].startswith('C'):
            r,q=map(int,g['name'][2:-1].split(','));values=cyclic_independent(r,q)
        else:values=fork_independent(g['name'])
        gamma,mu,cs,cost=values;assert cost>0 and cost<U
        assert gamma==F(g['gamma']) and mu==F(g['mu']) and cost==F(g['C'])
        assert cs==list(map(F,g['cs']))
        c1=cs[1%len(cs)];u=[cs[m%len(cs)]-m*(m+1)//2*c1 for m in range(221)]
        assert all(x.denominator==1 for x in u)
        prep.append((c1,list(map(int,u))))
    cert=json.load(open(ROOT/'efv_sharp_volume_dual.json'))
    assert cert['first']==11 and cert['max_degree']==220
    ucoef=[F(0)]*221;rhs=F(0)
    for row in cert['rows']:
        typ,*args=row['label'];t=F(row['multiplier']);assert t>=0
        if typ=='nonnegative':
            n=args[0];assert n>=11
            Q=n*(n+1)//2;ucoef[n]-=t/Q;rhs-=t*F(1-Q,Q)
        elif typ=='first':
            n=args[0];assert n==11
            Q=n*(n+1)//2;ucoef[n]-=t/Q;rhs+=t
        elif typ=='shift':
            n,m=args;assert m==11 and 1<=n<=209
            Q=(n+m)*(n+m+1)//2-n*(n+1)//2
            ucoef[n]+=t/Q;ucoef[n+m]-=t/Q;rhs+=t
        else:raise AssertionError(typ)
    assert [x['degree'] for x in cert['equalities']]==list(range(2,11))
    for row in cert['equalities']:
        n=row['degree'];t=F(row['multiplier']);ucoef[n]-=t;rhs+=t*(n*(n+1)//2-1)
    den=lcm(*(x.denominator for x in ucoef))
    ucoef=[(i,int(v*den)) for i,v in enumerate(ucoef) if v]
    residual=[-c1+F(sum(u[i]*v for i,v in ucoef),den) for c1,u in prep]
    assert min(residual)>=0 and rhs==F(31802,31801)
    assert min(residual)==F(cert['min_coefficient']) and rhs==F(cert['constant'])
    zeros=[g['name'] for g,v in zip(G,residual) if v==0]
    assert zeros==cert['zero_germs']
    result=dict(status='independent exact audit passed',local_types=len(G),cyclic_types=sum(g['name'].startswith('C') for g in G),fork_types=sum(g['name'].startswith('F') for g in G),positive_multipliers=len(cert['rows']),signed_equalities=len(cert['equalities']),minimum_residual=str(min(residual)),zero_residual_types=len(zeros),objective_lower_bound=str(rhs),volume_lower_bound=str(rhs-1),elapsed_seconds=time.time()-start)
    (ROOT/'efv_audit_result.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2));print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
if __name__=='__main__':main()
\end{filecontents*}

\begin{filecontents*}[overwrite]{efv_check_printed_table.py}
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Check all printed integer coefficients and an arithmetic control basket.
Python >=3.9, standard library only. Run without Python's -O option.
"""
from pathlib import Path
from fractions import Fraction as F
import json
from efv_local_catalogue import catalogue
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
tab=json.loads((ROOT/'efv_printed_table.json').read_text())
cert=json.loads((ROOT/'efv_sharp_volume_dual.json').read_text())
D=int(tab['denominator']);Q=lambda n:F(n*(n+1),2)
assert len(tab['rows'])==len(cert['rows'])==66
assert len(tab['equalities'])==len(cert['equalities'])==9
for row,old in zip(tab['rows'],cert['rows']):
    assert row['label']==old['label']
    lab=row['label'];n=lab[1]
    scale=Q(n) if lab[0]!='shift' else Q(n+lab[2])-Q(n)
    assert F(int(row['numerator']),D)==F(old['multiplier'])/scale>0
for row,old in zip(tab['equalities'],cert['equalities']):
    assert row['degree']==old['degree']
    assert F(int(row['numerator']),D)==F(old['multiplier'])
G=catalogue()
def a(g,n):return g['cs'][n%g['period']]
def u(g,n):return a(g,n)-Q(n)*a(g,1)
def row_value(g,lab):
    kind,n=lab[:2]
    return u(g,n+lab[2])-u(g,n) if kind=='shift' else u(g,n)
def row_rhs(lab):
    kind,n=lab[:2]
    if kind=='shift':return Q(n+lab[2])-Q(n)
    return Q(n)-1 if kind=='nonnegative' else Q(n)
res=[]
for g in G:
    val=-D*a(g,1)
    val-=sum(int(r['numerator'])*row_value(g,r['label']) for r in tab['rows'])
    val-=sum(int(r['numerator'])*u(g,r['degree']) for r in tab['equalities'])
    assert val>=0,(g['name'],val)
    res.append(val)
constant=sum(int(r['numerator'])*row_rhs(r['label']) for r in tab['rows'])
constant+=sum(int(r['numerator'])*(Q(r['degree'])-1) for r in tab['equalities'])
assert constant==D*F(31802,31801)
names={'C(49,17)','C(59,42)','C(22,13)'}
control=[g for g in G if g['name'] in names]
assert len(control)==3
V=-1-sum(a(g,1) for g in control)
rho=10-V-sum(g['gamma'] for g in control)
P=lambda n:1+Q(n)*V+sum(a(g,n) for g in control)
assert V==F(1,31801) and rho==2
assert [P(n) for n in range(1,12)]==[0]*10+[1]
assert all(sum(row_value(g,r['label']) for g in control)==row_rhs(r['label']) for r in tab['rows'])
assert all(res[i]==0 for i,g in enumerate(G) if g['name'] in names)
result={'status':'ALL PRINTED COEFFICIENTS AND CONTROL CHECKS PASSED',
        'common_denominator':str(D),'inequality_rows':66,'equality_rows':9,
        'local_types':len(G),'zero_residuals':sum(v==0 for v in res),
        'objective_constant':str(F(constant,D)),
        'control_volume':str(V),'control_picard_number':str(rho),
        'control_P_1_to_11':[str(P(n)) for n in range(1,12)]}
(ROOT/'efv_printed_table_result.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
\end{filecontents*}

\begin{filecontents*}[overwrite]{efv_local_catalogue.py}
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Exact local catalogue for exceptional del Pezzo surfaces with
mld>1/11 and P_1=...=P_10=0. Standard library only.
The accompanying paper proves completeness of the parameter ranges.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from math import gcd, lcm, ceil
from pathlib import Path
import json, time

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
S=10000
A=1328286
W=(2646679, 2502711, 2225722, 1676569, 1489733, 1041192, 678175, 458179, 302742, 39074, 0, 0, 0, 0)
U=F(12*A-sum(W),S)
KAP=F(99,100)
D=F(37,2)*A+sum(m*w for m,w in enumerate(W,1))
TAIL=ceil((D-100*KAP*S)/(A-121*KAP*S))
RCAP=121*TAIL
RBOUND=ceil(U/KAP)


def hj(r,q):
    z=[]
    while q:
        e=(r+q-1)//q;z.append(e);r,q=q,e*q-r
    return z


def cyclic_cost_numerator(r,q,ell,B):
    """Return r*S*C for a cyclic germ 1/r(1,q)."""
    qi=pow(q,-1,r);a=q+1;b=qi+1
    total=A*(r*(ell-B+3)-q-qi-3)
    sm=0
    for m,w in enumerate(W,1):
        sm+=(m*a+r-1)//r+(m*b)//r
        total+=w*(r*sm-m*(m+1)//2*(a+b))
    return total


def verify_infinite_cyclic_family():
    # Analytic tail: C > (A*ell-D)/S, r-1 <121ell-100.
    assert A-121*KAP*S>0
    assert (A-121*KAP*S)*TAIL-D+100*KAP*S>=0
    count=0
    for e in range(3,22):
      for r1 in range(1,22):
        if (e-2)*r1>=22:continue
        for q1 in ([0] if r1==1 else range(1,r1)):
          if gcd(r1,q1)!=1:continue
          left=hj(r1,q1);bl=sum(x-2 for x in left)
          inv=pow(q1,-1,r1) if r1>1 else 0
          ss=(q1*inv-1)//r1
          alpha=(e-1)*r1-q1
          for t in range(1,min(10,alpha)+1):
            low=max(r1,t);high=(RCAP-r1*t)//alpha
            if alpha>11:high=min(high,(r1*(11-t)-1)//(alpha-11))
            for n in range(low,high+1):
              if gcd(n,t)!=1 or(n==t and n!=1):continue
              h=n//t-1;v=n-(h+1)*t;tail=hj(t+v,v)
              ell=len(left)+1+h+len(tail)
              B=bl+e-2+sum(x-2 for x in tail)
              r=alpha*n+r1*t;q=((e-1)*inv-ss)*n+inv*t
              assert 0<q<r and gcd(q,r)==1 and 11*(n+r1)>r
              assert B<=19
              c=cyclic_cost_numerator(r,q,ell,B)
              assert c>0,('nonpositive local cost',e,r1,q1,t,n)
              if r>=30:
                  assert 100*c>=99*S*r*(r-1),('failed order bound',e,r1,q1,t,n)
              count+=1
    # Canonical A_{r-1}: C=(A/S)*(r-1/r) >=KAP*(r-1)>0.
    assert F(A,S)>KAP
    return count


def solve_linear(mat,vec):
    n=len(mat);a=[[F(x) for x in row]+[F(v)] for row,v in zip(mat,vec)]
    for j in range(n):
        k=next(k for k in range(j,n) if a[k][j])
        a[j],a[k]=a[k],a[j]
        v=a[j][j];a[j]=[x/v for x in a[j]]
        for i in range(n):
            if i==j or not a[i][j]:continue
            v=a[i][j];a[i]=[x-v*y for x,y in zip(a[i],a[j])]
    return [a[i][-1] for i in range(n)]


def fork(e0,arms):
    diagonal=[e0];edges=[]
    for r,q in arms:
        last=0
        for d in hj(r,q):
            j=len(diagonal);diagonal.append(d);edges.append((last,j));last=j
    n=len(diagonal);mat=[[0]*n for _ in range(n)]
    for i,d in enumerate(diagonal):mat[i][i]=d
    for i,j in edges:mat[i][j]=mat[j][i]=-1
    b=solve_linear(mat,[d-2 for d in diagonal])
    if min(1-x for x in b)<=F(1,11):return None
    gamma=n-sum((d-2)*x for d,x in zip(diagonal,b))
    sigma=sum(F(1,r) for r,q in arms)-1
    eta=e0-sum(F(q,r) for r,q in arms)
    group=4*eta/sigma**2
    mu=gamma+1-1/group
    period=lcm(*(x.denominator for x in b))
    cs=[]
    for m in range(period):
        f=[(m+1)*x-((m+1)*x).__floor__() for x in b]
        cs.append((sum((d-2)*x for d,x in zip(diagonal,f))-sum(d*x*x for d,x in zip(diagonal,f))+2*sum(f[i]*f[j] for i,j in edges))/2)
    cost=F(A,S)*mu+sum(F(w,S)*cs[(i+1)%period] for i,w in enumerate(W))
    return dict(name=f'F({e0};{arms})',gamma=gamma,mu=mu,C=cost,period=period,cs=cs,ell=n)


def fork_bases():
    out=[]
    for e in range(3,7):
      for r in range(2,10):
        for q in range(1,r):
          if gcd(r,q)==1 and (e-1)*r-q<=10:
            z=fork(e,[(2,1),(2,1),(r,q)])
            if z is not None:out.append((z,None))
    for d in range(2,11):
      for t in range(1,d):
        if gcd(d,t)==1:
          z=fork(2,[(2,1),(2,1),(d+t,t)])
          if z is not None:out.append((z,(d,t)))
    for e in (2,3):
      for r in (3,4,5):
        for q2 in (1,2):
          for q3 in range(1,r):
            if gcd(r,q3)!=1 or(r==3 and q2>q3):continue
            z=fork(e,[(2,1),(3,q2),(r,q3)])
            if z is not None:out.append((z,None))
    assert len(out)==63
    assert all(z['C']>0 for z,_ in out)
    # D-II extensions: C_u >=C_1+(A/S)(u-1); hence u>=3 cannot satisfy C<U.
    assert min(z['C'] for z,_ in out)+2*F(A,S)>U
    # Canonical D: ell>=4, group>=8, so C>=(A/S)*(5-1/8)>U.
    assert F(A,S)*F(39,8)>U
    return out


def cyclic(r,q):
    ch=hj(r,q);ell=len(ch);B=sum(d-2 for d in ch)
    p0,p1=r,q+1;dis=[]
    for d in ch:
        dis.append(F(p1,r));p0,p1=p1,d*p1-p0
    assert p1==r
    if min(dis)<=F(1,11):return None
    qi=pow(q,-1,r)
    if q>qi:return None
    cost=F(cyclic_cost_numerator(r,q,ell,B),S*r)
    if cost>=U:return None
    gamma=ell-sum((d-2)*(1-a) for d,a in zip(ch,dis))
    mu=gamma+1-F(1,r)
    period=r//gcd(r,q+1);cs=[F(0)];sm=0
    for m in range(1,period):
        sm+=(m*(q+1)+r-1)//r+(m*(qi+1))//r
        cs.append(F(r*sm-m*(m+1)//2*(q+qi+2),r))
    assert cost==F(A,S)*mu+sum(F(w,S)*cs[(i+1)%period] for i,w in enumerate(W))
    return dict(name=f'C({r},{q})',gamma=gamma,mu=mu,C=cost,period=period,cs=cs,ell=ell)


def catalogue():
    assert RBOUND>=30
    out=[]
    for r in range(2,RBOUND+1):
      for q in range(1,r):
        if gcd(r,q)!=1:continue
        z=cyclic(r,q)
        if z is not None:out.append(z)
    cyclic_n=len(out)
    for z,params in fork_bases():
        if z['C']<U:out.append(z)
        if params is not None:
            d,t=params;zz=fork(2,[(2,1),(2,1),(2*d+t,d+t)])
            if zz is not None and zz['C']<U:out.append(zz)
    print("catalogue counts",cyclic_n,len(out)-cyclic_n,flush=True)
    assert max(g['period'] for g in out if g['name'].startswith('F'))<=27
    return out
\end{filecontents*}

\begin{filecontents*}[overwrite]{efv_verify_nonvanishing.py}
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Verify local positivity and b(X)<=29 using exact arithmetic only.
Python >=3.9; standard library only. See the accompanying manuscript for the geometric inputs
and the proof that the parameter ranges and analytic tail are exhaustive.
The dual certificates are read from efv_nonvanishing_dual.json and
first_33_dual.json, first_35_dual.json,...,first_66_dual.json, and
first_{30,31,32,34}_integrality_duals.json and efv_small_volume_duals.json next to
this script. The latter certifies focused nonvanishing at volume <=1/31801.
Run from any directory: python3 /path/to/efv_verify_nonvanishing.py
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from math import gcd, lcm, ceil
from pathlib import Path
import json, time

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
S=10000
A=1332160
W=(2626097,2448046,2149838,1750133,1451008,1087041,736627,487293,306241,112769,42854,0,0,0)
U=F(12*A-sum(W),S)
KAP=F(99,100)
D=F(37,2)*A+sum(m*w for m,w in enumerate(W,1))
TAIL=ceil((D-100*KAP*S)/(A-121*KAP*S))
RCAP=121*TAIL


def hj(r,q):
    z=[]
    while q:
        e=(r+q-1)//q;z.append(e);r,q=q,e*q-r
    return z


def cyclic_cost_numerator(r,q,ell,B):
    """Return r*S*C for a cyclic germ 1/r(1,q)."""
    qi=pow(q,-1,r);a=q+1;b=qi+1
    total=A*(r*(ell-B+3)-q-qi-3)
    sm=0
    for m,w in enumerate(W,1):
        sm+=(m*a+r-1)//r+(m*b)//r
        total+=w*(r*sm-m*(m+1)//2*(a+b))
    return total


def verify_infinite_cyclic_family():
    # Analytic tail: C > (A*ell-D)/S, r-1 <121ell-100.
    assert A-121*KAP*S>0
    assert (A-121*KAP*S)*TAIL-D+100*KAP*S>=0
    count=0
    for e in range(3,22):
      for r1 in range(1,22):
        if (e-2)*r1>=22:continue
        for q1 in ([0] if r1==1 else range(1,r1)):
          if gcd(r1,q1)!=1:continue
          left=hj(r1,q1);bl=sum(x-2 for x in left)
          inv=pow(q1,-1,r1) if r1>1 else 0
          ss=(q1*inv-1)//r1
          alpha=(e-1)*r1-q1
          for t in range(1,min(10,alpha)+1):
            low=max(r1,t);high=(RCAP-r1*t)//alpha
            if alpha>11:high=min(high,(r1*(11-t)-1)//(alpha-11))
            for n in range(low,high+1):
              if gcd(n,t)!=1 or(n==t and n!=1):continue
              h=n//t-1;v=n-(h+1)*t;tail=hj(t+v,v)
              ell=len(left)+1+h+len(tail)
              B=bl+e-2+sum(x-2 for x in tail)
              r=alpha*n+r1*t;q=((e-1)*inv-ss)*n+inv*t
              assert 0<q<r and gcd(q,r)==1 and 11*(n+r1)>r
              assert B<=19
              c=cyclic_cost_numerator(r,q,ell,B)
              assert c>0,('nonpositive local cost',e,r1,q1,t,n)
              if r>=30:
                  assert 100*c>=99*S*r*(r-1),('failed order bound',e,r1,q1,t,n)
              count+=1
    # Canonical A_{r-1}: C=(A/S)*(r-1/r) >=KAP*(r-1)>0.
    assert F(A,S)>KAP
    assert count==1694986
    return count


def solve_linear(mat,vec):
    n=len(mat);a=[[F(x) for x in row]+[F(v)] for row,v in zip(mat,vec)]
    for j in range(n):
        k=next(k for k in range(j,n) if a[k][j])
        a[j],a[k]=a[k],a[j]
        v=a[j][j];a[j]=[x/v for x in a[j]]
        for i in range(n):
            if i==j or not a[i][j]:continue
            v=a[i][j];a[i]=[x-v*y for x,y in zip(a[i],a[j])]
    return [a[i][-1] for i in range(n)]


def fork(e0,arms):
    diagonal=[e0];edges=[]
    for r,q in arms:
        last=0
        for d in hj(r,q):
            j=len(diagonal);diagonal.append(d);edges.append((last,j));last=j
    n=len(diagonal);mat=[[0]*n for _ in range(n)]
    for i,d in enumerate(diagonal):mat[i][i]=d
    for i,j in edges:mat[i][j]=mat[j][i]=-1
    b=solve_linear(mat,[d-2 for d in diagonal])
    if min(1-x for x in b)<=F(1,11):return None
    gamma=n-sum((d-2)*x for d,x in zip(diagonal,b))
    sigma=sum(F(1,r) for r,q in arms)-1
    eta=e0-sum(F(q,r) for r,q in arms)
    group=4*eta/sigma**2
    mu=gamma+1-1/group
    period=lcm(*(x.denominator for x in b))
    cs=[]
    for m in range(period):
        f=[(m+1)*x-((m+1)*x).__floor__() for x in b]
        cs.append((sum((d-2)*x for d,x in zip(diagonal,f))-sum(d*x*x for d,x in zip(diagonal,f))+2*sum(f[i]*f[j] for i,j in edges))/2)
    cost=F(A,S)*mu+sum(F(w,S)*cs[(i+1)%period] for i,w in enumerate(W))
    return dict(name=f'F({e0};{arms})',gamma=gamma,mu=mu,C=cost,period=period,cs=cs,ell=n)


def fork_bases():
    out=[]
    for e in range(3,7):
      for r in range(2,10):
        for q in range(1,r):
          if gcd(r,q)==1 and (e-1)*r-q<=10:
            z=fork(e,[(2,1),(2,1),(r,q)])
            if z is not None:out.append((z,None))
    for d in range(2,11):
      for t in range(1,d):
        if gcd(d,t)==1:
          z=fork(2,[(2,1),(2,1),(d+t,t)])
          if z is not None:out.append((z,(d,t)))
    for e in (2,3):
      for r in (3,4,5):
        for q2 in (1,2):
          for q3 in range(1,r):
            if gcd(r,q3)!=1 or(r==3 and q2>q3):continue
            z=fork(e,[(2,1),(3,q2),(r,q3)])
            if z is not None:out.append((z,None))
    assert len(out)==63
    assert all(z['C']>0 for z,_ in out)
    # D-II extensions: C_u >=C_1+(A/S)(u-1); hence u>=3 cannot satisfy C<U.
    assert min(z['C'] for z,_ in out)+2*F(A,S)>U
    # Canonical D: ell>=4, group>=8, so C>=(A/S)*(5-1/8)>U.
    assert F(A,S)*F(39,8)>U
    return out


def cyclic(r,q):
    ch=hj(r,q);ell=len(ch);B=sum(d-2 for d in ch)
    p0,p1=r,q+1;dis=[]
    for d in ch:
        dis.append(F(p1,r));p0,p1=p1,d*p1-p0
    assert p1==r
    if min(dis)<=F(1,11):return None
    qi=pow(q,-1,r)
    if q>qi:return None
    cost=F(cyclic_cost_numerator(r,q,ell,B),S*r)
    if cost>=U:return None
    gamma=ell-sum((d-2)*(1-a) for d,a in zip(ch,dis))
    mu=gamma+1-F(1,r)
    period=r//gcd(r,q+1);cs=[F(0)];sm=0
    for m in range(1,period):
        sm+=(m*(q+1)+r-1)//r+(m*(qi+1))//r
        cs.append(F(r*sm-m*(m+1)//2*(q+qi+2),r))
    assert cost==F(A,S)*mu+sum(F(w,S)*cs[(i+1)%period] for i,w in enumerate(W))
    return dict(name=f'C({r},{q})',gamma=gamma,mu=mu,C=cost,period=period,cs=cs,ell=ell)


def catalogue():
    assert U/KAP<282
    out=[]
    for r in range(2,283):
      for q in range(1,r):
        if gcd(r,q)!=1:continue
        z=cyclic(r,q)
        if z is not None:out.append(z)
    cyclic_n=len(out)
    for z,params in fork_bases():
        if z['C']<U:out.append(z)
        if params is not None:
            d,t=params;zz=fork(2,[(2,1),(2,1),(2*d+t,d+t)])
            if zz is not None and zz['C']<U:out.append(zz)
    assert cyclic_n==2063 and len(out)==2114
    assert max(g['period'] for g in out if g['name'].startswith('F'))<=27
    return out


def verify_dual(G):
    cert=json.loads((ROOT/'efv_nonvanishing_dual.json').read_text())
    M=cert['B'];y=list(map(F,cert['y']));z=list(map(F,cert['z']));d=F(cert['delta'])
    assert M==66 and len(y)==M-1 and len(z)==3 and all(a>=0 for a in z) and d>=0
    vals=[]
    for g in G:
        cs=[g['cs'][m%g['period']] for m in range(M+1)];c1=cs[1]
        u=[c-m*(m+1)//2*c1 for m,c in enumerate(cs)]
        assert all(v.denominator==1 for v in u)
        E=[u[2]]+[u[m+1]-2*u[m]+u[m-1] for m in range(2,M)]
        UB=[c1,g['gamma']-c1,g['mu']-F(4,3)*c1]
        val=sum(a*b for a,b in zip(y,E))+sum(a*b for a,b in zip(z,UB))+d*g['C']
        assert val>=0,(g['name'],val)
        vals.append(val)
    rhs=[F(2)]+[F(1)]*(M-2)
    upper=[F(-1),F(10),F(40,3)]
    total=sum(a*b for a,b in zip(y,rhs))+sum(a*b for a,b in zip(z,upper))+d*U
    assert total<0
    assert total==F(cert['constant']) and min(vals)==F(cert['min_coefficient'])
    return dict(B=M,number_of_types=len(G),min_coefficient=str(min(vals)),contradictory_upper_bound=str(total))


def verify_first_section_duals(G):
    """Verify direct first-degree exclusions and integral-dimension chains.

    Rows are expanded from definitions, denominator-cleared, and checked on
    every catalogue column. A chain may use a zero only after a preceding
    certificate excludes P_n>=1. Since P_n is a nonnegative integer, this
    proves that the new zero is valid. The final certificate has no branch.
    """
    M=220;Q=[m*(m+1)//2 for m in range(M+1)]
    prepared=[]
    for g in G:
        c1=g['cs'][1%g['period']]
        u=[g['cs'][m%g['period']]-Q[m]*c1 for m in range(M+1)]
        assert all(v.denominator==1 for v in u)
        prepared.append(([int(v) for v in u],c1,g['gamma'],g['mu'],g['C']))

    def check(cert,first,known_zeros=(),forbidden=None,volume_ceiling=None):
        assert cert['first']==first and cert['max_degree']==M
        wu=[F(0)]*(M+1);wc=F(0);wg=F(0);wm=F(0)
        wcost=F(cert['cost_correction']);assert wcost>=0
        rhs=wcost*U
        for row in cert['rows']:
            label=row['label'];t=F(row['multiplier']);assert t>=0
            kind=label[0]
            if kind=='eq':
                j,sgn=label[1:];assert 0<=j<first-2 and sgn in (-1,1)
                t*=sgn
                if j==0:wu[2]+=t;rhs+=2*t
                else:
                    n=j+1;wu[n+1]+=t;wu[n]-=2*t;wu[n-1]+=t;rhs+=t
            elif kind=='base':
                j=label[1];assert j in (0,1,2)
                if j==0:wc+=t;rhs-=t
                elif j==1:wg+=t;wc-=t;rhs+=10*t
                else:wm+=t;wc-=F(4,3)*t;rhs+=F(40,3)*t
            elif kind=='nonneg':
                n=label[1];assert first<=n<=M
                wu[n]-=t/Q[n];rhs+=t*F(1-Q[n],Q[n])
            elif kind=='first':
                assert label[1]==first;wu[first]-=t/Q[first];rhs-=t
            elif kind=='shift':
                n,m=label[1:];assert m==first and 1<=n<=M-m
                dq=Q[n+m]-Q[n];wu[n+m]-=t/dq;wu[n]+=t/dq;rhs-=t
            elif kind=='zero':
                n,sgn=label[1:];assert n in known_zeros and sgn in (-1,1)
                wu[n]+=sgn*t/Q[n];rhs+=sgn*t*F(Q[n]-1,Q[n])
            elif kind=='nonzero':
                n=label[1];assert n==forbidden and first<n<=M
                wu[n]-=t/Q[n];rhs-=t
            elif kind=='volume_ceiling':
                assert volume_ceiling is not None and F(label[1])==volume_ceiling
                wc-=t;rhs+=t*(1+volume_ceiling)
            elif kind=='cost':
                wcost+=t;rhs+=t*U
            else:raise AssertionError(('unknown row type',label))
        denominator=lcm(*(x.denominator for x in wu+[wc,wg,wm,wcost]))
        int_u=[(i,int(v*denominator)) for i,v in enumerate(wu) if v]
        int_c,int_g,int_m,int_cost=[int(x*denominator) for x in (wc,wg,wm,wcost)]
        vals=[]
        for g,(u,c1,gamma,mu,cost) in zip(G,prepared):
            val=F(sum(w*u[i] for i,w in int_u))+int_c*c1+int_g*gamma+int_m*mu+int_cost*cost
            assert val>=0,('first-degree certificate failed',first,forbidden,g['name'],val)
            vals.append(val/denominator)
        assert rhs<0 and rhs==F(cert['constant']) and min(vals)==F(cert['min_coefficient'])
        return dict(first=first,forbidden_nonzero=forbidden,active_rows=len(cert['rows']),min_coefficient=str(min(vals)),contradictory_upper_bound=str(rhs))

    direct=[]
    for first in [33]+list(range(35,67)):
        cert=json.loads((ROOT/f'efv_first_{first}_dual.json').read_text())
        direct.append(check(cert,first))
    chains=[]
    for first in (30,31,32,34):
        chain=json.loads((ROOT/f'efv_first_{first}_integrality_duals.json').read_text())
        assert chain['first']==first and chain['max_degree']==M
        zeros=chain['forced_zero_degrees'];certs=chain['certificates']
        assert len(certs)==len(zeros)+1 and len(set(zeros))==len(zeros)
        known=[];results=[]
        for n,cert in zip(zeros,certs[:-1]):
            assert first<n<=M and cert['forbidden_nonzero']==n
            results.append(check(cert,first,known,n));known.append(n)
        assert certs[-1]['forbidden_nonzero'] is None
        results.append(check(certs[-1],first,known))
        chains.append(dict(first=first,forced_zero_degrees=zeros,certificates=results))
    assert len(direct)==33 and sum(len(c['certificates']) for c in chains)==34
    small=[]
    certificates=json.loads((ROOT/'efv_small_volume_duals.json').read_text())
    assert [c['first'] for c in certificates]==list(range(12,30))
    for cert in certificates:
        cap=F(cert['volume_ceiling']);assert cap==F(1,31801)
        small.append(check(cert,cert['first'],volume_ceiling=cap))
    return direct,chains,small


def main():
    start=time.time()
    count=verify_infinite_cyclic_family()
    G=catalogue()
    result=verify_dual(G)
    result['preliminary_bound']=result.pop('B')
    result['B']=29
    result['first_degree_exclusions'],result['integrality_chains'],result['small_volume_exclusions']=verify_first_section_duals(G)
    result['small_volume_general_b_bound']=13
    result['small_volume_high_mld_b_bound']=11
    result['small_volume_ceiling']='1/31801'
    low_mld_volumes=list(map(F,['2/10465','9/37835','1/5083','4/13685','2/17043','127/596505','16/61617','127/596505','2/17043','2/36855','1/21385','2/14053','1/10465','1/10465','1/3115','6/26611','6/26611','1/3713','3/23621','8/35945','8/35945','3/23621','3/23621','3/23621','1/3289']))
    # The geometric classification is LS23 Theorem1.9/Table8; check its numerical minimum.
    assert len(low_mld_volumes)==25 and min(low_mld_volumes)==F(1,21385)>F(1,31801)
    result['classified_low_mld_minimum_volume']='1/21385'
    result['exceptional_small_volume_b_bound']=11
    result.update(cyclic_parameter_checks=count,tail_length=TAIL,r_cap=RCAP,cyclic_order_bound=282,cost_upper_bound=str(U),elapsed_seconds=round(time.time()-start,3),status='all exact assertions passed')
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
    (ROOT/'efv_nonvanishing_result.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')

if __name__=='__main__':main()
\end{filecontents*}

\begin{filecontents*}[overwrite]{efv_verify_sharp.py}
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Rebuild the complete catalogue and verify V >= 1/31801 exactly.
Run: python3 /path/to/efv_verify_sharp.py
Python >=3.9, standard library only; no solver and no input catalogue.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from pathlib import Path
import json,time
from efv_local_catalogue import verify_infinite_cyclic_family, fork_bases, catalogue, A,W,U,TAIL,RCAP,RBOUND
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
start=time.time()
count=verify_infinite_cyclic_family()
fb=fork_bases();G=catalogue()
assert len(G)==2121
assert sum(g['name'].startswith('C') for g in G)==2067
cert=json.loads((ROOT/'efv_sharp_volume_dual.json').read_text())
assert cert['first']==11 and cert['max_degree']==220
assert F(cert['lower_bound'])==F(1,31801)
M=cert['max_degree'];Q=[F(n*(n+1),2) for n in range(M+1)]
c1=[g['cs'][1%g['period']] for g in G]
u=[[g['cs'][n%g['period']]-Q[n]*c for g,c in zip(G,c1)] for n in range(M+1)]
res=[-c for c in c1];constant=F(0)
# For A n <= b, lambda>=0: objective >= -lambda*b + y*e
# if objective + lambda*A - y*E is coefficientwise nonnegative.
for item in cert['rows']:
 lab=item['label'];lam=F(item['multiplier']);assert lam>0
 if lab[0]=='nonnegative':
  n=lab[1];assert 11<=n<=M
  row=[-x/Q[n] for x in u[n]];rhs=(1-Q[n])/Q[n]
 elif lab[0]=='first':
  n=lab[1];assert n==11
  row=[-x/Q[n] for x in u[n]];rhs=F(-1)
 elif lab[0]=='shift':
  n,d=lab[1:];assert d==11 and n>=1 and n+d<=M
  dq=Q[n+d]-Q[n]
  row=[-(x-y)/dq for x,y in zip(u[n+d],u[n])];rhs=F(-1)
 else:
  raise AssertionError(('unexpected row',lab))
 res=[x+lam*a for x,a in zip(res,row)];constant-=lam*rhs
assert [item['degree'] for item in cert['equalities']]==list(range(2,11))
for item in cert['equalities']:
 n=item['degree'];y=F(item['multiplier'])
 res=[x-y*a for x,a in zip(res,u[n])];constant+=y*(Q[n]-1)
assert min(res)>=0
assert constant==F(31802,31801)==F(cert['constant'])
assert min(res)==F(cert['min_coefficient'])
zero=[g['name'] for g,x in zip(G,res) if not x]
assert zero==cert['zero_germs']
assert len(zero)==308
result=dict(status='ALL EXACT CHECKS PASSED',theorem='mld>1/11 and b=11 imply V>=1/31801 for exceptional del Pezzo surfaces',A=A,W=W,budget=str(U),analytic_tail=TAIL,cyclic_search_cap=RCAP,cyclic_order_bound=RBOUND,cyclic_parameter_checks=count,cyclic_types=2067,fork_types=54,local_types=len(G),inequality_dual_rows=len(cert['rows']),equality_dual_rows=len(cert['equalities']),exact_objective_bound=str(constant),volume_lower_bound='1/31801',zero_columns=len(zero),smallest_positive_column=str(min(x for x in res if x>0)),elapsed_seconds=time.time()-start)
(ROOT/'efv_sharp_result.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
\end{filecontents*}
